(.venv) $datasets = @(
  @{ file = "datasets\logic.txt"; depth = 5; timeout = 60 },
  @{ file = "datasets\nat.txt"; depth = 5; timeout = 60 },
  @{ file = "datasets\lists.txt"; depth = 6; timeout = 90 },
  @{ file = "datasets\sets.txt"; depth = 6; timeout = 60 },
  @{ file = "datasets\hol_main_easy_goals_test.txt"; depth = 6; timeout = 90 },
  @{ file = "datasets\hol_main_mid_goals_test.txt"; depth = 8; timeout = 120 },
  @{ file = "datasets\hol_main_hard_goals_test.txt"; depth = 10; timeout = 180 }
)
PS C:\Users\astan\Desktop\llm-isabelle-main>
PS C:\Users\astan\Desktop\llm-isabelle-main>
(.venv) foreach ($model in $models) {
  foreach ($ds in $datasets) {
    $file = $ds["file"]
    $depth = $ds["depth"]
    $timeout = $ds["timeout"]

    if (Test-Path $file) {
      Write-Host "`n=== NO-SLEDGE | $file | $model ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan

      python -m prover.experiments bench --file $file --beam 3 --max-depth $depth --timeout $timeout --reranker both --quickcheck --nitpick --model $model --shuffle --seed 42

      Write-Host "`n=== SLEDGE | $file | $model ===" -ForegroundColor Green

      python -m prover.experiments bench --file $file --beam 3 --max-depth $depth --timeout $timeout --reranker both --sledge --quickcheck --nitpick --model $model --shuffle --seed 42

      Write-Host "`n=== AGGREGATING CURRENT RESULTS ===" -ForegroundColor Yellow
      python -m prover.experiments aggregate --dir datasets\results --min-rows 1
    }
    else {
      Write-Host "Skipping missing dataset: $file" -ForegroundColor Yellow
    }
  }
}
